# Changelog

All notable changes to Heinzelmännchen, newest first. Versions follow [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org); entries are generated by [git-cliff](https://git-cliff.org) from [conventional commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org).

## [0.9.53](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.53) - 2026-11-21

## [0.9.45](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.45) - 2026-11-15

One kind of chat with streaming replies, AI models chosen by level, a cleaner desktop and browser experience, and Heinzelmännchen now requires Python 3.15. Supersedes 0.9.44, whose desktop build failed.

### 🐛 Bug fixes

- Package media-player.js in the desktop app ([#834](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/834)) ([34ad745](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/34ad7454d898d562666e0f6663d68836bf4c18b6))

## [0.9.44](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.44) - 2026-11-15

One kind of chat with streaming replies, AI models chosen by level, a cleaner desktop and browser experience, and Heinzelmännchen now requires Python 3.15.

### ✨ Features

- One kind of chat, models by level, Python 3.15 (0.9.44) ([#831](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/831)) ([5efeb8c](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/5efeb8c1fb8df161bda8d309073f3bc06796cf7b))

## [0.9.43](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.43) - 2026-11-15

Plugins can now bring their own AI models: each plugin's models appear as an AI access option of their own and in the chat model picker. Documents can use several connectors at once, and Heinzel can send its notices and questions to your chat app as an extra place to answer them.

### ✨ Features

- **ai:** Plugin models, connectors and chat self channel (0.9.43) ([#826](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/826)) ([6e21ff8](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/6e21ff86dec4b215f852b04c237da47cc49503ba))

### 🐛 Bug fixes

- **chat:** Bound the self-chat wait; pin consumers for 0.9.43 ([#829](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/829)) ([f0d7352](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/f0d7352d85df877907419c55e3cd1fe3ba3cf977))

### 🏗️ Build and CI

- Let the Python lane's selection name every test it needs ([#825](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/825)) ([1d984e4](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/1d984e40f37c2f8ab5b93265dee40ff344cad764))

## [0.9.42](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.42) - 2026-11-14

Chat works again with Claude Code 2.1.x: Heinzel checks that your Claude Code version is in the supported range and explains in plain language when it is not.

## [0.9.23](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.23) - 2026-11-06

On Apple silicon Macs, use `hz app install` to install the verified desktop app and `hz app uninstall` to remove it. Manage preview features in Settings.

Automatic updates give macOS more time to acknowledge a daemon restart. If an earlier update was interrupted, check `hz system daemon status`. Recovery support for the specific 0.9.21-to-0.9.22 interruption requires verified incident evidence. Confirm the daemon runs a new scheduled check before treating it as recovered.

## [0.9.16](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.16) - 2026-11-05

0.9.16 adds CLI-created release-draft workflows, calendar views and reminders, profile onboarding, optional-feature health, offline removal and supervised web serving. Settings search stays readable on phones and compact layouts. Source access review waits until the current reply settles. Update actions stay in place when status details load. Google/Slack/GitHub companion publication and real-provider acceptance remain separate. Activity-detail scrolling remains under investigation.

Legacy macOS 0.9.14/0.9.15 daemons may require one-time verified retirement and reinstall. After 0.9.16 publication, run `uv run --no-project --with heinzel==0.9.16 python -I -m heinzel.heads.daemon_recovery plan`. Review the entire plan; historical profile ownership remains unknown. Repeat with `retire --confirm PLAN_SHA256`, using the exact current plan digest. After successful retirement, use `hz system update --source 'heinzel==0.9.16'`, then `hz system daemon install`; separately choose `hz ui install` for supervised web serving. Preserve the existing profile; never purge data or bypass refusals. Recovery is macOS-only; see docs/DAEMON_RECOVERY.md. Future ordinary supervised updates remain intended in place. Native migration acceptance is pending; historical automatic-handover and WebKit incidents are not claimed fixed.

### ✨ Features

- **web:** Restart the web server from settings ([#499](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/499)) ([bda5c43](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/bda5c438d6b30893892f6ee1680c0e88165ffbce))

- Add release poems, calendar and offline system removal ([#511](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/511)) ([7d8c9bf](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/7d8c9bf30afa9b8ad3c74d00f95a22f9cfe048bc))

## [0.9.15](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.15) - 2026-11-04

Helper notifications now identify who spoke with a name and hat. Run the web app as a supervised background service, use control and navigation keys in the phone terminal, and share the app through the native share sheet. Phone workspace controls stay usable as content loads and the keyboard changes the viewport. The release also adds a quick local terminal overview, supports keeping important footer information visible, reports damaged notification storage, and links Learn more to the first-session guide.

## [0.9.13](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.13) - 2026-11-02

hz can now keep itself and its plugins up to date. Automatic updates are on by default: when a newer release is ready, a small notice counts down 30 seconds, you can move it back an hour, and then hz updates and restarts in place. Turn it off in the footer's update pane or in Settings. It never updates in the middle of a chat or a helper job, and never while you develop hz from a source checkout. Also fixed: the terminal follows your theme, the footer clock popup no longer repeats itself, the Tailscale sharing messages say what to do, and an open window reconnects after a restart without asking you to sign in again.

### ✨ Features

- **web:** Update hz and plugins automatically, with postpone ([#452](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/452)) ([6943732](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/69437321ef32bdd7da2ad8f90a681fe09e2860c0))

## [0.9.11](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.11) - 2026-11-01

A plugin preview now says which version it replaces, so changing a version no longer reads like a new install. This release also carries everything from 0.9.10 with its acceptance checks passing, including daemon install from the app, sign-in that survives a restart, soundscapes that play when picked, and plugins that keep their versions when another one changes.

## [0.9.10](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.10) - 2026-11-01

Install and everyday use are smoother. The daemon installs from the app with real progress, an installed app stays signed in after a restart, and an old daemon left behind no longer stops an install. Picking a soundscape plays it right away. Setup offers the four roles and asks for a team name. Adding or removing a plugin no longer upgrades your other plugins, and the app offers Restart and apply when plugins change outside it.

### ✨ Features

- **dx:** Add a small-change golden path and scope pre-push checks ([#339](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/339)) ([411055d](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/411055d83f393e56de7dbc6f3086622cb229927a))

- **plugin:** Offer Restart and apply after outside changes ([#381](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/381)) ([f26b9c6](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/f26b9c60f5b8dabb4c7a6cac520daf3055e92d84))

## [0.9.9](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.9) - 2026-10-31

This release makes installing Heinzelmännchen dependable again. The installer now finds the latest app through a validated release redirect instead of the rate-limited anonymous GitHub API, reports which step failed instead of a generic error, and a macOS reinstall after `uv tool uninstall` works when a stopped, exactly trusted service remains. Plugin playbooks may also declare optional chat starter drafts: these are inert and neither read sources nor schedule or send anything.

### 🛡️ Security

- **security:** Record Sonar remediation lessons ([#311](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/311)) ([3190096](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/3190096a8765fc25e44dd800ee2d8d13de3a3e66))

## [0.9.7](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.7) - 2026-10-30

Scheduled agents can use separately reviewed item-based notification policies to suppress repeated attempts across overlapping reads and restarts. CLI discovery emits the actual executable command tree and schemas without running operations. Interrupted local checks now stop their owned child processes, and the plugin import guard avoids unnecessary package scans. Item identities remain provider-reported, and fixed reminders retain their prior consent.

### ✨ Features

- **heinzel:** Review observed item notification policies ([#291](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/291)) ([7aa9798](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/7aa979878c584020e3f2fccd2e23ef7875310565))
- **cli:** Describe the executable command tree ([#285](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/285)) ([536d293](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/536d293d9e8290902f17aa66bff8004a2271a070))

### 🐛 Bug fixes

- **ci:** Stop owned steps on interruption ([#289](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/289)) ([2cb85d1](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/2cb85d107bed1567200a3c8a20947abfa5891f1b))

### ⚡ Performance

- **ci:** Avoid unnecessary plugin package scans ([#293](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/293)) ([621370f](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/621370f08900b1ddfd684775b85dd0777bc4166c))

## [0.9.6](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.6) - 2026-10-30

Agent chats can suggest exact connected sources for separate review and approval, then compare whole results from permitted reads. The Electron shell now asks for document-scoped notification and microphone consent and revokes it on navigation. These changes do not establish complete connected workflows, individual-event history or real OS permission and delivery.

### ✨ Features

Durable Heinzel chats now retain identity, styling and proposed contracts across reloads. Review private contract, appearance and skill drafts in chat, follow recorded tool activity, and explicitly resolve interrupted turns. Scheduled connected workflows and complete Electron distribution remain in development.

## [0.9.1](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.9.1) - 2026-10-29

Plugins now own complete features across CLI, API, MCP and UI through public extension contracts, including menu priorities, Today widgets, notifications and soundscapes. Standard installations include Clock, Resource, Tailscale and the Heinzelmännchen theme; sharing and playback require an explicit choice.

Authors get minimal and rich scaffolds, matching human and agent guides for public or private repositories, and one independently released toolkit for owning checks, builds, preview, installed-wheel acceptance and immutable publication. Plugin-only work completes its cycle without running Core's suite.

This is a breaking plugin boundary. Start with a fresh installation and configuration.

## [0.4.0](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.4.0) - 2026-10-24

hz becomes an app you live in: a Claude-style web UI you can install to the Dock, with a chat that acts on hz once you confirm, a terminal, a notification center and desktop notifications from the daemon. Plugins bring themes (type, density, fonts) and can be installed from the app.

## [0.3.0](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.3.0) - 2026-10-24

A web UI, onboarding, and integrations through the vendors' own CLIs. hz ui opens a local web app: navigation and search, settings, the contract graph, team roster, inventory, doctor and an AI chat. hz join <team repo> recognises you from your gh and Google logins and matches the team roster; hz team creates and manages team repositories, and hz org sync applies the team's recommendations you accept. GitHub, Atlassian, Google Workspace (Docs, Sheets, Drive, Calendar, Gmail), Zoom (from its summary emails) and mail are first-party plugins that work as an ordinary employee through gh, acli and gws. Plugins bring playbooks with template contracts; contracts form a graph (hz workflow graph) and can look ahead; drafts expire after 90 days. hz system changelog --newer shows what a release brings before you update.

### 🛡️ Security

- **security:** Refuse commit emails made from the machine's hostname ([#14](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/14)) ([fa6bdd3](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/fa6bdd36c2937adc489b361f588ec11e19317635))
- **security:** Attach in-toto provenance; fuzz the contract parser ([#23](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/23)) ([fb2e490](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/fb2e490771f54e1d41f5642c7b8722657387b809))

## [0.2.0](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.2.0) - 2026-10-24

Heinzelmännchen now works for you while you're away. A daemon on your machine runs jobs on a schedule and leaves
a receipt for every run; workflow contracts, Markdown files with frontmatter, compile to those jobs. Modules declare
the capabilities they need (`tracker:read`, `documents:read,write`) and configuration binds adapters to them; agent
runners for Claude Code and any command-line agent draft the results. Credentials live in the OS keychain, `hz` tells
you when a newer release exists, and nothing is sent home. Adapters for GitHub and Atlassian ship as plugins.

### ✨ Features

- **system:** Check for a newer release ([#8](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/8)) ([0ce849f](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/0ce849f2469d253a245ed8c8591ae6934b735a5e))
- **system:** Credentials in the OS keychain ([#6](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/6)) ([be8bdc6](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/be8bdc6cd5a86236cd3b11f93c0f7d4e33a19318))
- **system:** Local daemon head with jobs, ticks and receipts ([#7](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/7)) ([2b160b2](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/2b160b28ddf88bbc73d9cbe2648ba6446cdb2e3f))
- **kernel:** Capability protocols, dependencies and agent runners ([#9](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/9)) ([700780e](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/700780ec4078243bdfa8f514a1e061de9f83f463))
- **workflow:** Contracts compile to daemon jobs ([#11](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/pull/11)) ([b673212](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/b67321229f712be40a6537fd98c5928629507d9f))

## [0.1.0](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/releases/tag/v0.1.0) - 2026-10-24

The first release: the foundation the workflows will build on. A kernel that discovers modules and the lets they
advertise; every operation as a CLI command, an MCP tool and a REST endpoint; layered configuration, health checks
and plugin management with a template and a first-party `clock` plugin; agent skills for coding agents; and releases
with provenance attestations and an SBOM. What comes next is in the [roadmap](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/blob/main/INTENT.md#where-we-are).

### ✨ Features

- Heinzelmännchen foundation ([b40f8eb](https://github.com/helmut-hoffer-von-ankershoffen/heinzel/commit/b40f8eb09950070326ca21765b9a2ceca180d2a5))
